Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The protocols operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 430. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 948 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the protocols space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 430 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 948. QED.
